===== PAGE 14 [page-0a707966e1c5dcdeb86cf12b] =====
--- seg-84b4a481e01a5dc96a01f983 [needs-review] ---
INTRODUCTION - In the early 1970s, strange phenomena began to occur along the coast and inland of the states of Pará and Maranhão in Brazil. News of these events spread across all regions, and the testimonies were unanimous: mysterious beams of light, projected by unidentified flying objects (UFOs), mostly appearing at night, that would attack men and women. According to reports, these UFOs would drain the blood of their victims, giving rise to the expression "Chupa-chupa" (Suck-suck), by which the phenomenon became popularly known. In other regions, particularly in Maranhão, it was known as "aparelho."

Despite a long incubation period, this phenomenon manifested with force and consistency never seen before in the history of Brazilian ufology. Within a few weeks, the news spread throughout Pará and Maranhão, shocking the population and creating an atmosphere of terror and collective panic. Driven by curiosity and with the aim of unraveling the mystery of Chupa-chupa, we embarked on a research project that took us to various municipalities in Pará. However, due to our limited financial resources and time constraints, we were unable to cover the entire states of Pará and Maranhão. We hope that in the near future, someone else will complete this research.

What we present in this article is not a fabrication by rural inhabitants but a faithful account of the facts and testimonies we collected. We listened attentively to each interviewee and selected the most coherent reports, which, when combined with other testimonies and the credibility of the sources, provided us with a clear picture. The goal of this work is to provide a realistic overview and a historical account of one of the most impressive UFO manifestations we have encountered.
--- seg-f49d755a45b359e9799591e3 [needs-review] ---
THE NEWSPAPERS ANNOUNCE - The first part of this research consists of a bibliographic survey of all articles and reports published in the Para state newspapers such as A Província do Pará, O Liberal, and Estado do Pará. Due to the reasons described above, it was not possible to perform the same service with the Maranhão press. We read all the reports from the aforementioned newspapers and felt that the Paraense press reported the Chupa-chupa phenomenon in a very interesting manner. All the reports were well-illustrated and conducted at the sites of incidence, alongside the

President of the Center for Linguistic Research (FCLLI) since its foundation, he currently serves on the Board of the Amazonian UFO Group (GIM), Caixa Postal 624, Belém (PA).

===== PAGE 15 [page-0586ff11a0a01e85cb822659] =====
--- seg-13b4d6fd347c8b2ad33a7d0f [needs-review] ---
**Testemunhas...**

Hoje, podemos reconstituir o fenômeno UFO conhecido como "Chupa-chupa", com base em relatos e notícias da época. Diversas notícias ufológicas precederam a onda Chupa-chupa, mas a mais significativa foi divulgada no jornal *O Liberal*, em 16 de julho de 1977:

"Um UFO fotografado em Montevideo, de forma esférica, tem sido comparado aos estranhos objetos vistos em diversos pontos do território paraense, como o lado maranhense do rio (Jurtipi e ao longo da região fronteiriça Pará-Maranhão). Ontem, esses objetos foram observados em diversas localidades do interior maranhense, causando pânico."

**Relatos e Investigação:**

Alguns dias antes, em 12 de julho de 1977, o *Jornal da Líria* noticiou, de forma clara, o que viria a ser o Fenômeno Chupa-chupa:

"Objeto voador que emite luz intensa e suga sangue das pessoas circula de boca em boca entre a população dos municípios de Bragança, Vizeu e Acará, no Pará, onde muitas pessoas foram vistas com o seio esquerdo ferido, parecendo ter sido sugado."

Em meses seguintes, em 8 de outubro, o *O Liberal* lançou a primeira de uma série de reportagens sobre o fenômeno, descrevendo o ataque a Aurora Nascimento, residente em Passagem Tabauruja, que tentou segurar o objeto, mas foi sugada pela luz.

**Detalhes do Fenômeno:**

A reportagem inicia-se de forma taxativa:

"Um estranho fenômeno, denominado 'Chupa-chupa', vem acontecendo há várias semanas no município de Vigia, mais exatamente em Santo Antônio do Imbitu, a cerca de 7 quilômetros da rodovia PA-140."

O médico Orlando Zoghbi examinou a paciente Aurora, confirmando o ataque de um objeto não identificado, que imobiliza as pessoas com uma luz intensa, causando pânico entre os moradores de Imbitu e Km-25, além de outras localidades.

**Depoimentos e Conclusões:**

A paciente Aurora descreveu o incidente:

"Eu fiquei apavorada. Chamei minha mãe. Enquanto ela chegava, uma forte luz veio e me envolveu. Senti furadas muito finas em meu seio, e desmaiei."

O Dr. Zoghbi, ao examinar as marcas, concluiu que se tratava de um episódio de histeria e pânico coletivo, produzido pela psicose coletiva, com o objeto conhecido pelos moradores como "Bicho Sugador" ou "Objeto Voador Não Identificado".

**Relatos Adicionais:**

Uma das vítimas, a senhora Rosito Ferreira, casada, com 62 anos, residente no Ramal dos Gados, relatou em 20 de outubro de 1977, que foi atacada por um UFO arredondado, que deixou marcas profundas em seu corpo, sem deixar vestígios de arranhões.

O pesquisador e estudioso do Fenômeno UFO, professor Fábio Zerpa, analisou esses relatos e observou que fatos semelhantes ocorreram no sul da Argentina na década de 1970, onde uma mulher foi atacada por uma entidade que lhe tirou sangue. Ele compara esses eventos com as mitologias japonesas dos "kapus", entidades que, segundo a lenda, vêm de uma estrela distante e habitam rios e mares.

"During the Chupa period, I would often go out at night to visit friends and relatives. Colares Island, located about 70 kilometers northeast of Belém, is separated from the continent by the Guajará-Mirim River. It is rich in UFO sightings and urological cases.

One night, I felt a strange 'heat' on my body, and when I looked, I saw a craft flying low. It was a ball of light, and as it grew brighter, I felt intense heat."

SECRET REPORTS - Since the rumors of the curious UFO appearances
began in the early Paraense and Maranhão, accompanied by the panic of the
people, the ° Air Command Regional (9 COM AR), based in Belém,
conducted an exhaustive field operation with the intent; to elucidate
the enigma of the Phenomenon.

CONCLUSIONS - This research, apart from the phenomenon, the ° Air
Command Regional (9 COM AR), based in Belém, allowed us, despite its
limitations, an adequate sampling of the Paraense and Maranhão UFO
wave in the 1970s. We highlight some points:

Chupa-chupa. The command order was as follows:

I) The area of action was northern Brazil,

1) The UFO phenomenon deserves frequent appearances near rivers, lakes and the Atlantic Ocean;

2) All information regarding the phenomenon must be collected;

3) Public statements and comments about the subject should be avoided. At the time, the Air Force sent researchers to various municipalities where UFOs were detected, collecting hundreds of reports and around 200 photographs of strange objects, UFOs. The most frequent appearance sites were the Bay of the Sun (Mosqueiro Island) and Colares Island (Pará). In these regions, numerous UFO photographs were obtained, most of them at night, and some short films recording the movements of the ships. Most of the photos were taken with professional cameras (Cannon, Nikon) equipped with teleobjectives, using 1000 ASA Kodak black-and-white film, equipment belonging to the Armed Forces. Upon completion of the field investigations,
the second phase of the operation was initiated, which
included the preparation of a report and
analysis of the collected data. This report,
approximately 300 pages in length, containing photographs of UFOs, drawings, maps, interview cards, and local newspaper clippings, was sent to the General Staff of the Armed Forces (EMFA), along with all existing photographic documentation (prints, negatives, photocopies). The results obtained throughout this

Authentic engraving from the Helan era,
work indicate that the Chupacabra phenomenon was widespread and of an ufological nature.

These beings, similar to those seen in Pará,
have the same appearance and objectives.

--- seg-231e93776656fcc8fe478006 [needs-review] ---
REGION - Describing the elegant
curve towards the west, after discharging into
the ocean at the level of the 10th avenue, São
Francisco heads south, in search of its sources,
until it merges with its alleys that spring from
the buttresses of the Canastra Mountains, already
in the vast plains of Belo Horizonte, the Minas
Gerais capital.
This fluvial semi-circle isolates, between the
Central Plateau and the Atlantic, a vast region
whose relief is characterized by a central ridge
that begins in the south with the Espinhaço
Mountains and extends to the north by the famous
Diamantina Plateau.
Magical region of high mountains and deep
valleys, abundant water and beautiful rock
formations, it was the pole that magnetized the
ancient miners who carved trails in search of
diamonds and emeralds. Recently, fascinated by
the natural beauty of its towns, beautiful
waterfalls, rich flora and varied fauna, the
federal authorities created the Diamantina
Plateau National Park, which forms the center
of a triangle whose vertices are the towns of
Morro do Chapéu to the north, Guanambi to
the west, and Icóé to the east. And it is within
this area that we will also locate a "very hot"
ufology site, not to mention that the Alvorada
Project located two of its Stations, whose
main function was to establish contact with
alien elements.
The main ufological occurrence here is
characterized by the observation of nocturnal
lights around which a real folklore has been
created among the population, with diverse
opinions ranging from phenomena attributed to
the existence of minerals, to the designation
of "planets", "apparatuses" and "discs", among
the more enlightened.
Several cases have been researched by me
in recent years and here I have selected the
most interesting ones, which I will now narrate.
2

INTRUSO - In late 1981, returning home early in the evening, the researcher Roque Santana de Araújo, was driving a new Corcel with several relatives and friends. They were on a dirt road connecting the municipality of Utinga to Morro do Chapéu, cities distant from Salvador by approximately four hundred kilometers.

Suddenly, observing his car, they noticed a strong luminosity moving through a brushwood that bordered the road. Intrigued by the sight, as they were unaware of any secondary roads that would allow traffic of trucks or tractors, they continued their journey, speculating about the strange light following them. Suddenly, in a sudden movement, the light approached from the right, flooding everything with intense brightness. Mr. Roque Santana, in a quick impulse, took the wheel from his son and pressed the accelerator hard. The car

Unidentified flying object (UFO) sightings have been reported in various parts of the world, often leaving witnesses in awe and mystery. These mysterious celestial phenomena have sparked countless debates and speculations, ranging from alien visitation to atmospheric anomalies.

The term UFO is used to describe any aerial object that cannot be immediately identified or explained. It does not inherently imply extraterrestrial origin, but rather serves as a placeholder for unknowns. Reports of UFOs vary greatly, from bright, disk-shaped objects hovering silently to fast-moving lights with erratic patterns.

Many UFO sightings remain unexplained, fueling the imagination and curiosity of both scientists and the general public. While some cases can be attributed to natural phenomena, aircraft, or weather conditions, others remain shrouded in mystery, leaving room for speculation and further investigation.

# Introdução

**Comentários sobre as Atividades Ufológicas Oficiais da FAB**

A Força Aérea Brasileira (FAB), como muitas outras organizações em todo o mundo, tem uma longa história de ligação com o fenômeno UFO. É natural que, dada a natureza sensacionalista do assunto, muitos países tenham mantido um certo nível de sigilo em relação às suas investigações ufológicas. No entanto, no Brasil, durante a época dourada da "invasão extraterrestre" na década de 1960, a FAB estabeleceu o Sistema de Investigação de Objetos Aéreos Não Identificados (SIOANI), o primeiro órgão oficial dedicado a estudar o fenômeno UFO na América Latina.

## O Nascimento do SIOANI

Em 1969, um grupo de oficiais da FAB, liderado pelo então Major José Vaz da Silva, então chefe do 4º Comando Aéreo Regional (COMAR) em São Paulo, criou o SIOANI dentro das instalações da FAB. O objetivo era organizar uma equipe de pesquisadores e informantes, tanto militares quanto civis, para investigar avistamentos de objetos voadores não identificados (OVNI) e coletar informações de diversas bases aéreas, clubes e associações privadas dedicadas à ufologia.

## A Publicação Inicial

O momento decisivo foi o lançamento de uma carta informativa, em março de 1969, pelo próprio Major Vaz da Silva, representando o SIOANI. A publicação delineava o primeiro estatuto e diretrizes da entidade, distribuindo tarefas e responsabilidades entre seus membros.

## O Crescimento do SIOANI

Em apenas cinco meses de funcionamento, o SIOANI lançou sua segunda publicação, um boletim informativo mais extenso, detalhando seus primeiros resultados e métodos. A resposta pública foi significativa, com o SIOANI recebendo um grande número de relatos de avistamentos de OVNIs de todo o Brasil.

## Conclusão

A criação do SIOANI marcou um ponto crucial na história da ufologia brasileira, proporcionando um fórum oficial para a investigação e discussão do fenômeno UFO. A abordagem inicial do SIOANI, combinando métodos científicos e uma rede de informantes, estabeleceu um padrão para futuras pesquisas ufológicas em todo o país.

What nature are the phenomena that have been observed in our most varied parts of the globe? Were they psychological, meteorological, astronomical? It is our duty to investigate their origins. Without the shadow of doubt, we must discover who is responsible for these occurrences, and organizations of a technical and cultural nature should not, cannot, hide themselves, casting shadows on the problem of OANI, which is growing increasingly intense.

Observations that are honest, related to the incidences of this phenomenon, show us that its frequency and diffusion, in various countries, have increased significantly.

A group of scientists, dedicated to the study of OANI, has been enriched with names of high professional category, which indicates the necessity of a scientific study; many occurrences have been reported by credible sources, prompting us to pay attention to this phenomenon.

We know that in many cases, the personal observations of those who report these occurrences may be influenced by "imagination"; but, in all, we must investigate. There is a fact, a new fact, a compelling factor that needs to be explained adequately. It is precise to conduct a methodical, scientific investigation. We must not start from the premise of non-existence of OANI. We cannot be certain. Any extreme position is unscientific.

Admitting the "possibility" of the existence of OANI justifies the scientific quest to investigate it. To penetrate the core of the phenomenon, investigating its psychiatric, psychological, sociological, astronomical, meteorological, legal, and other aspects, constitutes a necessity.

And it is there that the position lies: "Mft-that locates the Ministry of Aeronautics, through the Command of the 4th Air Zone, where the Central Investigation of Unidentified Aerial Objects (CIOANI) is organized."

The Second SIOANI Bulletin

Observation: The text that follows is an integral, genuine transcription
                from Issue #2 of the Unidentified Aerial Objects Investigation System
                report, accurately as it was identified in August 1969.
                Given its official nature, we have chosen to preserve its exact
                structure and grammatical presentation.
                Moreover, the orthographic, grammatical, and accent marks
                have been maintained as they originally appeared.

SYSTEM OF INVESTIGATION OF NON-IDENTIFIED AEROSPACE OBJECTS
--- seg-beb07226a93244e1b94be589 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Presentation
i
And this is Bulletin No. 2 which was published last March. Our fifth month of this General Quarter does not appear, it is printed, the rhythm we desired.
We are few, very few in this General Quarter, but the messages that come to us are so intense, demanding each and every excess of effort and attention to overcome the numerical deficiency.
Entering, our companions designated for the accomplishment of the OANI mission, without prejudicing their specific missions, continue in the same rhythm of enthusiasm and dedication to this supplementary task.
The area of our investigations has enlarged; the quadrants of our NIOANI and IOANI are increasing, and the level of our XONANI is indicating a cultural, economic and social fix compatible with the founded and ongoing investment series.
This is why we know that FAB exists.

While studying and researching such a palpitant subject, it becomes evident that the future of our society, education, and primarily moral formation, will not permit a possible and even probable confusion with what margins.

Confidently in FAB,
elements categorized have been emerging from anonymity and are now presenting as pre-
conceptions of our investigations.

In this Bulletin
2, we publish our relation of our NIOs, of our IOAs, and initiate a relation of cases that, since last October of 1968, have caught our attention and are prompting us to study something within our possibilities.
We have, therefore, revised this Bulletin, commemorating the memorable feat of Apollo 11 and its astronauts, transforming the Moon into a field of use for our race.
Congratulations to the three cosmic heroes of this century, congratulations to the American people, who so clearly, so lucidly, so transparently express their value of democracy, and express the greatness of that nation which has placed a man on the Moon.
On the occasion of the 50th anniversary of Santos Dumont's death, the pioneer of flight, we join the 25th anniversary of July 1969, the conquest of our romantic satellite, both dates to be celebrated with the continuation of the efforts of man in the pure representation of his quality on the vast Earth of his God.

ufos have been reported in various parts of the world for centuries, with many cases remaining unsolved. while some ufo sightings can be explained by natural phenomena or human technology, there are still instances where the objects observed defy conventional explanations. these cases often spark speculation about extraterrestrial life and the possibility of ufo visits.

investigators and scientists approach ufo reports with skepticism, emphasizing the importance of thorough evidence and analysis. they encourage witnesses to provide detailed accounts, including descriptions of the ufo, its movements, and any unusual phenomena. this information is then carefully evaluated to determine if there is a plausible natural or human-made explanation.

despite the skepticism, many people continue to believe in ufos and their potential connection to extraterrestrial civilizations. they point to the vastness of the universe and the likelihood that intelligent life may exist elsewhere. some even suggest that ufos may be exploring our planet, gathering information, or even making first contact.

while ufo sightings remain a subject of fascination and debate, the search for definitive answers continues. advancements in technology and scientific understanding may one day provide more insights into these mysterious objects and their origins.

===== PAGE 92 [page-9d57b3ddeff6da11bc72299c] =====
--- seg-2941d8afd004a94ecd53e0b6 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Unidentified flying object (UFO) sightings have been reported throughout history, with various cultures and civilizations documenting these mysterious phenomena. From ancient texts to modern-day accounts, people have described seeing strange objects in the sky that defy conventional explanations.

While some UFO sightings can be attributed to natural phenomena, aircraft, or weather conditions, many remain unexplained. These unexplained observations have fueled speculation and fascination with the possibility of extraterrestrial life.

The term "UFO" is used to describe any aerial object that cannot be immediately identified. It does not imply that the object is of extraterrestrial origin, but rather that its nature remains unknown. The study of UFOs, or "unidentified flying objects," involves examining reports, analyzing evidence, and investigating potential causes to better understand these enigmatic occurrences.

Final Considerations
ACIAN, through the NIANs, PIANIs, IANs and even other fonts, aims to systematically investigate and verify the occurrence of an OANi phenomenon, determining its investigation, pursuing verification, identification, and study of something that deserves attention.

In this case, the verification and examination of individuals, objects, and locations are carried out within a pre-established criterion, without loss, but with the necessary flexibility for adaptations in the research.

Among the examined situations, with great weight, the psychiatric; various "manifestations" are selected, notably those cases of wealth that demand a significant and extreme investigation.

Among the cases presented in this Bulletin, those that are considered invalid, or that exist, lead us to admit a great possibility, indeed, evidence that suggests the presence of "voluntary ideations," closely related to specific details that should be presented to demonstrate their veracity. Some of these cases are also examples of proven personal schizophrenia (hallucinatory or delusional).

In a scientific investigation, as this one, we must submit a studied minimization, carefully examining all collected data; in ACIAN, with a confidential character, we make an effort to organize each type of OANi phenomenon in our experience.

This is not the case of this Bulletin, where the cases were mixed, as if the manifestations were presented separately from the knowledge of the SIOANI and from those who, within a reserve criterion, should have recognized them as work by the Brazilian Air Force.

Some documents of extreme use by the SIOANI

Part V
Correspondences of SIOANI received by Brazilian groups
Three correspondences are presented here, received from private groups of Brazilian ufologists, which were forwarded to the Centro para Pesquisas de Voos Espaciais (CPDV) in November 1984, containing a brief description of the history of SIOANI and its current level of activity. The first correspondence was a response to a citation from the CPDV itself regarding the dissemination of information and documents concerning SIOANI, and warned that such actions were done at a reserved level.

In the second correspondence, dated December 1988, sent by the public relations service of the Ministry of Aeronautics to ufologist Irene Granchi, the Ministry acknowledges the importance of the ufological issue, citing the existence of an organization (a newly created one, with different norms from SIOANI) specializing in the problem within the company. Unfortunately, this correspondence does not mention the name of the organization, its responsible person, or address.

Finally, the third correspondence, sent in November 1989, was addressed to the late Paulist ufologist Osni Schwarz, consultant to the CPDV, clearly identifying the existence of an entity engaged in the national ufological movement, the Núcleo do Comando de Defesa Aérea Brasileira (NUCOMDABRA), located in Brasília.

Moreover, this correspondence indicates that the ufologist Osni Schwartz, in a post as a material, information, and cooperation provider, was not only acknowledged but also welcomed as a formal member of the Joint's detailed activities.
